{"forms":[{"form":"maddahs","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Arabic diacritic similar in appearance to a tilde. Over an alif (آ (ʔā) \"alif maddah\"), it indicates glottal stop, /ʔ/, followed by a long /aː/. The diacritic is not used separately, only over alif. Alif maddah can appear in the beginning: آسِف (ʔāsif) or in the middle of a word: قُرْآن (qurʔān)."]},{"glosses":["In the Qur'an, it indicates an extra long vowel and can appear over any long vowel."]},{"glosses":["In Persian the letter is used only in the beginning of a word with a long /aː/: آمدن (âmadan)."]}],"upstream_line":4750895,"upstream_offset":11246054675,"upstream_record_sha256":"85f3aa28b63f1c8d1f958b5c659e412bc623a2f9b9b65851fc642de77b2b3565","word":"maddah"}
